问题与改进

每条按等级与类型标注，展开可看触发条件、实际观察、验收标准、证据原文与可直接粘贴的上游 Issue 草稿。评审发现不等于本轮实际阻塞，也不因业务通过而标为已解决。

独立评测发现

来源：独立评审 · 口径 v2 · 源 Run 39068146241 / attempt 1

最新上游状态：未复核。原评审“已解决”不作为上游修复证明；应用绕行、业务 QA 通过均不关闭框架问题。

等级与类型怎么定
  • 严重阻塞交付，或造成数据丢失、安全问题，且没有合理绕行
  • 重要按指引或公开 API 正常使用会静默得到错误结果，或只能用非公开手段绕行
  • 轻微能完成，但要多花排查成本或在应用侧绕行
  • 信息措辞、示例完整度等不影响结果的改进
  • 实现缺陷实现违背文档或公开约定
  • 能力缺口需求属于框架职责，但缺少能力或公开接入
  • 指引错误指引、示例或注释写错，或彼此矛盾，照做会出错
  • 指引缺失指引缺少必要说明或示例，需要读源码或试探
  • 易用性改进现有能力可用，但有改进空间

轻微指引缺失上游未复核反馈复核：证据支持原评审置信度：confirmed
F7 · Transaction guidance omits the single-connection SQLite blocking failure mode

文档 / Skill / 示例 · @nocobase/db-sqlite · app/.agents/skills/nocobase-db/SKILL.md · 原评审：未解决

⌄

反馈证据核对

The frozen DB Skill's transaction section only warns that outer db.query()/repository()/connection() calls run outside the transaction and do not roll back (E49). It never says that on the default single-connection SQLite pool such a read waits on the connection the transaction holds, so the request looks hung. The dialect wrapper only adds decimal aggregates in configurePool (E50), and the db client hands configurePool config.pool ?? {} (E73) while the app declares SQLite without a pool override (E74), so no pool size or acquire timeout is configured. The reopen endpoint was restructured for exactly this reason: the manager lookup was moved after the transaction, with a comment that the reader would wait for the only SQLite connection (E65). Verify-2 recorded the reopen POST hanging over 30 seconds (E63), and Verify-3, after that change, records reopen returning the ticket to In progress (E72). This establishes a real guidance gap rather than an application defect: the rollback warning does not deter a read that needs no rollback, and no other frozen guidance covers the single-connection block (E75, E51).

  • 职责与公开约定：The Skill's transaction contract is about transaction scoping and rollback: it says to use only the callback connection and that outer calls run outside the transaction and will not roll back. It contains no statement about SQLite's single pooled connection, waiting for a connection, acquire timeout, or a hung request. Because this is a guidance-gap improvement, the applicable commitment is the Skill's stated purpose of preventing transaction mistakes; it does not cover this failure mode. A general 'use only the connection' sentence is not the same as explaining that an outer read blocks. E49
  • 实际行为证据：The SQLite driver's configurePool only wraps afterCreate to install decimal aggregates and sets neither pool size nor acquire timeout (E50). The db client passes the driver configurePool(config, config.pool ?? {}) (E73), and the application's SQLite connection declares only dialect, filename, schemaManagement and debug (E74), so defaults remain. The dialect's transaction subclass handles failed COMMIT/release state (E51) but does not prevent an outer reader from waiting for the transaction's connection. Thus the implementation leaves the blocking behavior described by the finding in place while the guidance warns only about rollback. E50 E73 E74 E51
  • 应用接入与业务边界：The final application code documents the exact failure: the manager lookup was moved after the transaction because a reader takes another connection while the transaction holds the only SQLite connection, so asking inside it waits for a lock that never frees (E65). Verify-2 observed POST /api/service-desk/tickets/2/reopen pending for over 30 seconds and still Resolved after reload (E63). Verify-3, with the lookup after the transaction, records reopen succeeding and the ticket returning to In progress (E72). The application therefore hit the pattern and fixed it; there is no evidence the final build still misuses the API, and the finding's claim that reopen hit exactly this is corroborated by the code move plus the before/after QA results. E65 E63 E72
  • 环境因素：The app's default and only declared connection is local SQLite via @nocobase/db-sqlite with a storage filename (E74); the blocking is a local pool/lock interaction, not an external service, credential or network dependency. The same reopen flow that hung in Verify-2 (E63) succeeds in Verify-3 after the lookup was moved (E72), which rules out a persistent network or deployment explanation for that hang. No environment material is missing for this narrow guidance claim. E74 E63 E72
  • 工厂因素：The omission is in the factory-shipped DB Skill itself (E49), not in an application-specific prompt or QA script. No factory template, injection configuration or build hook is needed to observe it: the Skill is the guidance artifact under review, and its transaction section is where the SQLite blocking warning and example would belong. No counter-evidence in the frozen factory materials shows another prompt supplying that warning. E49
  • 已有能力与推荐用法：The existing public guidance does not already cover this: the DB Skill's transaction section stops at rollback semantics (E49), and the application-development database-connections reference covers SQLite paths and configuration but not single-connection blocking or acquire timeouts (E75). The SQLite transaction patch protects COMMIT failure state only (E51), not a nested outer read. Searches of the frozen skills and the db-sqlite package found no documented pool or blocking guidance, so the improvement is not already available through an existing entry point. E49 E75 E51

反馈复核是对原始反馈的证据核对，不等于人工确认、上游修复状态或自动修复授权；保留原评审置信度、分类与处理状态。

The DB skill warns that an outer db.query()/repository()/connection() inside a transaction runs outside it and will not roll back. It does not warn that on SQLite, where the pool has one connection, such a read waits for the transaction connection until acquire timeout and makes the request appear hung. The SQLite wrapper sets no acquire timeout, and reopen hit exactly this.

实际影响
An agent can follow the rollback warning and still ship a request that hangs for the full pool timeout; the failure looks like a network or UI stall rather than a transaction bug.
建议改法
Add an explicit SQLite single-connection warning and fail-fast example: never perform an outer read inside a transaction; resolve ids after commit or use the callback connection. Consider a bounded acquire timeout with an actionable error.
诊断 · 触发条件、应有行为、绕行代价与验收标准
触发条件
Call an outer repository/query read inside database.transaction(...) on default SQLite.
应有能力 / 行为
Guidance states that the call blocks on the only pooled connection until acquire timeout and recommends moving it after commit or using the callback connection.
实际观察
The skill only warns about rollback semantics, and the SQLite wrapper leaves the pool/acquire timeout at defaults, so reopen hung over 30 seconds.
应用绕行与代价
Move the outer read after the transaction; this is what the application eventually did.
改进后的验收标准
DB/SQLite guidance contains the blocking warning and a before/after example, and a regression test asserts reopen completes without a pool timeout.
评审时证据 · 冻结快照 4 条
app/.agents/skills/nocobase-db/SKILL.md:282–299E49

The DB transaction guidance warns that outer db.query()/repository()/connection() calls inside a transaction do not roll back. It does not warn that on a single-connection SQLite pool such a call blocks until the pool acquire timeout and looks like a hung request.

## 5. Transactions

```ts
await db.transaction(async (connection) => {
  await connection.repository('orders').createOne({ values: order });
  await connection.query
    .updateTable('inventory')
    .set({ reserved: true })
    .where('sku', '=', order.sku)
    .execute();
});
```

Use only the `connection` the callback hands you. Calling the outer `db.query()`, `db.builder()`, `db.repository()` or `db.connection()` inside the callback runs outside the transaction and will not roll back with it — the same applies to metadata work, which must go through the transaction connection's `collectionMetadata` and `collections`.

Returning commits; throwing rolls back. Swallowing an error inside the callback and continuing commits the partial work.

A Repository nested write opens its own transaction when none is active and joins an existing one when there already is, without a savepoint per write. Several independent calls that must roll back together need an explicit transaction around them.
packages/@nocobase/db-sqlite/dist/src/index.js:154–172E50

SQLite configurePool only wraps afterCreate for decimal aggregates; it does not set pool size or an acquire timeout. The single-connection default therefore remains in force.

    configurePool: (_config, pool) => {
        const afterCreate = pool.afterCreate;
        return {
            ...pool,
            afterCreate: (connection, done) => {
                try {
                    installDecimalAggregates(connection);
                    if (afterCreate) {
                        afterCreate(connection, done);
                    }
                    else
                        done(null, connection);
                }
                catch (error) {
                    done(error);
                }
            },
        };
    },
app/server/service-desk/service.ts:628–633E65

The application comment records the reopen-hang root cause: the manager lookup was moved after the transaction because a reader takes another connection while the transaction already holds the only SQLite connection.

    // Looked up after the transaction: a reader takes another connection, and
    // the transaction already holds the only one a single-connection database
    // (SQLite) allows, so asking inside it waits for a lock that never frees.
    const managers = await this.userIdsWithPermissionSet(
      'service-desk-manager',
    );

上游 Issue 草稿 · Markdown
### [轻微][指引缺失] Transaction guidance omits the single-connection SQLite blocking failure mode

**相关对象**：`@nocobase/db-sqlite`（评审时 0.1.0-beta.4）、`app/.agents/skills/nocobase-db/SKILL.md`

**反馈证据核对**：反馈复核：证据支持

The frozen DB Skill's transaction section only warns that outer db.query()/repository()/connection() calls run outside the transaction and do not roll back (E49). It never says that on the default single-connection SQLite pool such a read waits on the connection the transaction holds, so the request looks hung. The dialect wrapper only adds decimal aggregates in configurePool (E50), and the db client hands configurePool config.pool ?? {} (E73) while the app declares SQLite without a pool override (E74), so no pool size or acquire timeout is configured. The reopen endpoint was restructured for exactly this reason: the manager lookup was moved after the transaction, with a comment that the reader would wait for the only SQLite connection (E65). Verify-2 recorded the reopen POST hanging over 30 seconds (E63), and Verify-3, after that change, records reopen returning the ticket to In progress (E72). This establishes a real guidance gap rather than an application defect: the rollback warning does not deter a read that needs no rollback, and no other frozen guidance covers the single-connection block (E75, E51).

- 职责与公开约定：The Skill's transaction contract is about transaction scoping and rollback: it says to use only the callback connection and that outer calls run outside the transaction and will not roll back. It contains no statement about SQLite's single pooled connection, waiting for a connection, acquire timeout, or a hung request. Because this is a guidance-gap improvement, the applicable commitment is the Skill's stated purpose of preventing transaction mistakes; it does not cover this failure mode. A general 'use only the connection' sentence is not the same as explaining that an outer read blocks.；证据：E49（app/.agents/skills/nocobase-db/SKILL.md:282–299）
- 实际行为证据：The SQLite driver's configurePool only wraps afterCreate to install decimal aggregates and sets neither pool size nor acquire timeout (E50). The db client passes the driver configurePool(config, config.pool ?? {}) (E73), and the application's SQLite connection declares only dialect, filename, schemaManagement and debug (E74), so defaults remain. The dialect's transaction subclass handles failed COMMIT/release state (E51) but does not prevent an outer reader from waiting for the transaction's connection. Thus the implementation leaves the blocking behavior described by the finding in place while the guidance warns only about rollback.；证据：E50（packages/@nocobase/db-sqlite/dist/src/index.js:154–172）、E73（packages/@nocobase/db/dist/src/database/internal/knex/client.js:1–20）、E74（app/server/config/database.ts:8–17）、E51（packages/@nocobase/db-sqlite/dist/src/transaction.js:1–39）
- 应用接入与业务边界：The final application code documents the exact failure: the manager lookup was moved after the transaction because a reader takes another connection while the transaction holds the only SQLite connection, so asking inside it waits for a lock that never frees (E65). Verify-2 observed POST /api/service-desk/tickets/2/reopen pending for over 30 seconds and still Resolved after reload (E63). Verify-3, with the lookup after the transaction, records reopen succeeding and the ticket returning to In progress (E72). The application therefore hit the pattern and fixed it; there is no evidence the final build still misuses the API, and the finding's claim that reopen hit exactly this is corroborated by the code move plus the before/after QA results.；证据：E65（app/server/service-desk/service.ts:628–633）、E63（artifacts/verify-2/browser-acceptance/report.json:4–49）、E72（artifacts/verify-3/browser-acceptance/report.json:35–35）
- 环境因素：The app's default and only declared connection is local SQLite via @nocobase/db-sqlite with a storage filename (E74); the blocking is a local pool/lock interaction, not an external service, credential or network dependency. The same reopen flow that hung in Verify-2 (E63) succeeds in Verify-3 after the lookup was moved (E72), which rules out a persistent network or deployment explanation for that hang. No environment material is missing for this narrow guidance claim.；证据：E74（app/server/config/database.ts:8–17）、E63（artifacts/verify-2/browser-acceptance/report.json:4–49）、E72（artifacts/verify-3/browser-acceptance/report.json:35–35）
- 工厂因素：The omission is in the factory-shipped DB Skill itself (E49), not in an application-specific prompt or QA script. No factory template, injection configuration or build hook is needed to observe it: the Skill is the guidance artifact under review, and its transaction section is where the SQLite blocking warning and example would belong. No counter-evidence in the frozen factory materials shows another prompt supplying that warning.；证据：E49（app/.agents/skills/nocobase-db/SKILL.md:282–299）
- 已有能力与推荐用法：The existing public guidance does not already cover this: the DB Skill's transaction section stops at rollback semantics (E49), and the application-development database-connections reference covers SQLite paths and configuration but not single-connection blocking or acquire timeouts (E75). The SQLite transaction patch protects COMMIT failure state only (E51), not a nested outer read. Searches of the frozen skills and the db-sqlite package found no documented pool or blocking guidance, so the improvement is not already available through an existing entry point.；证据：E49（app/.agents/skills/nocobase-db/SKILL.md:282–299）、E75（app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-development/references/database-connections.md:34–54）、E51（packages/@nocobase/db-sqlite/dist/src/transaction.js:1–39）

反馈复核是对原始反馈的证据核对，不等于人工确认、上游修复状态或自动修复授权；保留原评审置信度、分类与处理状态。

The DB skill warns that an outer db.query()/repository()/connection() inside a transaction runs outside it and will not roll back. It does not warn that on SQLite, where the pool has one connection, such a read waits for the transaction connection until acquire timeout and makes the request appear hung. The SQLite wrapper sets no acquire timeout, and reopen hit exactly this.

**影响**：An agent can follow the rollback warning and still ship a request that hangs for the full pool timeout; the failure looks like a network or UI stall rather than a transaction bug.

**建议改动**：Add an explicit SQLite single-connection warning and fail-fast example: never perform an outer read inside a transaction; resolve ids after commit or use the callback connection. Consider a bounded acquire timeout with an actionable error.

**验收标准**：DB/SQLite guidance contains the blocking warning and a before/after example, and a regression test asserts reopen completes without a pool timeout.

**评审时证据（冻结快照）**：
- `app/.agents/skills/nocobase-db/SKILL.md:282–299` — The DB transaction guidance warns that outer db.query()/repository()/connection() calls inside a transaction do not roll back. It does not warn that on a single-connection SQLite pool such a call blocks until the pool acquire timeout and looks like a hung request.
- `packages/@nocobase/db-sqlite/dist/src/index.js:154–172` — SQLite configurePool only wraps afterCreate for decimal aggregates; it does not set pool size or an acquire timeout. The single-connection default therefore remains in force.
- `app/server/service-desk/service.ts:628–633` — The application comment records the reopen-hang root cause: the manager lookup was moved after the transaction because a reader takes another connection while the transaction already holds the only SQLite connection.
- `artifacts/verify-2/browser-acceptance/report.json:4–49` — Verify-2 exercises the main flow but records a reopen request hanging over 30 seconds, missing dashboard awaiting/workload signals, no notification center or bell, and an unverifiable 24-hour reminder.

> 来源：nb3-factory 独立搭建评审（口径 v2，源 Run 39068146241 / attempt 1）。尚未复核最新上游源码。
业务实现与执行环境观察 · 不计入框架得分（7）
重要实现缺陷候选反馈：未复核原评审置信度：confirmed原评审：已解决
F1 · Service-desk policy lookup used a logical key instead of collection policies

Agent / 业务实现 · @nocobase/app-plugin-authorization

⌄

未提供反馈复核记录，保留为候选反馈；原评审置信度不代表已排除误报。

After admin sign-in the dashboard returned 403 PERMISSION_DENIED. The application expected a policy at decision.conditions.database[<composite logical id>], while database authorization produces policies keyed by actual collection ids (serviceDeskTickets, serviceDeskComments). A policy unit test then exposed a missing guard when no collection policy was present.

实际影响
Authorized managers and administrators saw permission-denied pages, and verify-1 could not exercise any service-desk flow.
建议改法
Keep collection policies keyed by collection id, deny explicitly when a required policy is absent, and retain the unit test for a decision without the collection policy.
诊断 · 触发条件、应有行为、绕行代价与验收标准
触发条件
Authorized dashboard or ticket request after login.
应有能力 / 行为
The app reads the serviceDeskTickets/serviceDeskComments policies and applies them to repository queries.
实际观察
The app looked up a composite logical key, found no policy, and returned 403; one test path threw a TypeError instead of a denial.
应用绕行与代价
Read policies by collection id and add an explicit missing-policy denial.
改进后的验收标准
Admin and manager can load dashboard and tickets; a decision without the expected collection policy yields a controlled denial and the policy test passes.
E20 E22 E67 E71 E62
重要实现缺陷候选反馈：未复核原评审置信度：confirmed原评审：已解决
F3 · Reopen hung because a nested read waited on the single SQLite connection

Agent / 业务实现 · @nocobase/db-sqlite · app/.agents/skills/nocobase-db/SKILL.md

⌄

未提供反馈复核记录，保留为候选反馈；原评审置信度不代表已排除误报。

Verify-2 observed POST /api/service-desk/tickets/2/reopen still loading after 30 seconds and the ticket remaining Resolved. The application performed a manager-id lookup through another database connection inside database.transaction(...); SQLite's pool holds the only connection for the transaction, so the nested read waited until the acquire timeout.

实际影响
Employees could not reopen an unresolved ticket, blocking a core acceptance branch.
建议改法
Move out-of-transaction reads after commit (as the final code does), keep all writes on the callback connection, and add a reopen regression test.
诊断 · 触发条件、应有行为、绕行代价与验收标准
触发条件
Reopen a resolved ticket on the single-connection SQLite configuration.
应有能力 / 行为
Reopen commits promptly, adds an activity entry and notifies the engineer/manager.
实际观察
The request blocked on a second connection until timeout and the status stayed Resolved.
应用绕行与代价
Resolve recipient ids after the transaction and use only the callback connection inside it.
改进后的验收标准
Reopen completes within a few seconds on SQLite, status becomes In progress, and activity/notification records are created.

F2 · Signed-in users had no employee access in the first delivery

Agent / 业务实现 · @nocobase/app-plugin-authorization · app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-plugin-users/SKILL.md

⌄

未提供反馈复核记录，保留为候选反馈；原评审置信度不代表已排除误报。

Verify-1 found both the factory admin and a newly registered account denied on the dashboard and ticket pages, with no user/role management entry. Authorization already adds the authenticated subject to every request, and Users supports assigning a Permission Set to that subject; the application had not seeded the default employee assignment.

实际影响
The main C01 flow could not start in verify-1; every account without an explicit role was denied.
建议改法
Seed the employee Permission Set for {type:'authenticated', id:'*'} and verify access with a freshly registered account, not only the factory administrator.
诊断 · 触发条件、应有行为、绕行代价与验收标准
触发条件
Sign in as administrator or register a new account in a fresh application.
应有能力 / 行为
Every signed-in account receives the employee baseline and can open the dashboard and its own tickets.
实际观察
The first seed did not assign the baseline to authenticated/*, so the pages denied access.
应用绕行与代价
Create the authenticated-subject assignment in the application seed and reload permissions.
改进后的验收标准
A newly registered account can submit and view its own ticket immediately after signup without manual role assignment.

轻微实现缺陷候选反馈：未复核原评审置信度：confirmed
F6 · Attachment picker accepted corrupt content and gave generic unsupported-file feedback

Agent / 业务实现 · @nocobase/app-plugin-file · app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-plugin-file/SKILL.md · 原评审：未解决

⌄

未提供反馈复核记录，保留为候选反馈；原评审置信度不代表已排除误报。

Verify-3 observed a 28-byte corrupt.png accepted and attached to ticket #3, while a .exe produced only a generic failure message. The File plugin documents that it has no content sniffing and that accept/maxSize/maxFiles are UI checks; the application checks only count and size and never validates extension, MIME or magic bytes.

实际影响
Users can attach unreadable or unexpected content, and unsupported files do not receive actionable feedback.
建议改法
Validate allowed extensions/MIME and file signatures in the application upload route before creating metadata, and return a field-level error naming the rejected file and reason.
诊断 · 触发条件、应有行为、绕行代价与验收标准
触发条件
Upload a corrupt PNG or unsupported executable as a ticket attachment.
应有能力 / 行为
The application rejects the file before storage with a clear per-file reason.
实际观察
The corrupt PNG was stored and linked to the ticket; the executable produced a generic request failure.
应用绕行与代价
Add server-side extension/MIME/signature validation in the app upload handler; optionally set accept for earlier browser feedback.
改进后的验收标准
Corrupt and unsupported files are rejected with specific feedback and create no attachment; valid PNG/PDF/DOCX still upload and render.

实现者过程与运行背景 · 0 个过程问题 / 2 条建议 · 不自动归为框架问题

本任务已处理只描述应用侧处理结果，不代表 NocoBase3 上游已修复。

本轮过程记录

Repaired service-desk authorization and bootstrap failures: admin and newly registered users now receive the intended permission sets, managers have a working user/role management page, and dashboard/ticket policy lookups use the actual composite collection ids. Verified with targeted type checks, lint, formatting, unit/component tests, fresh SQLite installation, an upgrade simulation, and an end-to-end API flow. The follow-up round repaired the assignee/filter select labels, the reopen deadlock (a nested user lookup inside the single-connection SQLite transaction), the missing dashboard unassigned/workload figures, and added the header notification bell plus an inbox navigation entry so assignment, resolution and overdue messages are reachable.

实现者未记录具体阻塞，不等于本轮没有问题。

实现者补充建议

来自可选过程笔记，不代表已独立确认；相同建议引用上方评审，其余保留原文。

建议 01 · testing

Test repair seeds on both fresh and upgraded schemas

可自动化

Seed 003 had to repair existing databases while leaving fresh installs unchanged. Fresh-only testing missed duplicate merged actions, and standalone loading caught a sibling-seed import that ordinary development did not.

建议改动

Add a seed test that loads each compiled seed in isolation and applies it to both an empty schema and a schema containing older permission-set grants and assignments.

来源：Agent · retro.json

建议 02 · authorization

Assert policy lookup keys against composed authorization policies

可自动化

The composite resource was queried with logical scope names, but runtime authorization policy maps are keyed by collection id.

建议改动

Keep the regression test that composes database policies and asserts ServiceDeskService reads serviceDeskTickets and serviceDeskComments, and use the same pattern for other composite resources.

来源：Agent · retro.json

Collection-level and record-level authorization for three service-desk roles, including user administration and default access for signed-in accounts.

E43

需求 → 框架职责 → 推荐与实际接入

Employee, engineer and manager access must differ by ticket ownership and assignment.直接支持
框架职责与业务边界
Authorization compiles permission-set grants into database conditions and scopes; repositories enforce the resulting policy.
推荐使用方式
Authorize a composite action, retain the returned collection policies, and bind them with withPolicy() on every ticket query.
Agent 实际使用
The final service indexes policies by serviceDeskTickets and serviceDeskComments and applies repository policies; QA verified employee isolation and engineer assignment.
缺口归因
未记录能力缺口
E39 E44 E67 E64
Every signed-in account should receive employee access without a separate role picker.直接支持
框架职责与业务边界
Authorization provides the authenticated subject; applications assign a default Permission Set to it.
推荐使用方式
Seed an assignment for {type:'authenticated', id:'*'} and keep role-specific assignments separate.
Agent 实际使用
The final seed assigns the employee set to authenticated/*; the first round omitted this, an application configuration gap recorded in F2.
缺口归因
未记录能力缺口
E40 E66 E62
Managers need a user-management surface and role assignment flow.正常组合
框架职责与业务边界
Users owns the API, invariants and role-scope registry; the application owns the page and role grants.
推荐使用方式
Build the page on UsersClient and register an application role scope; do not expect a bundled page.
Agent 实际使用
The final app ships client/pages/users/index.tsx and server role-scope wiring, matching the documented ownership.
缺口归因
未记录能力缺口

E48

需求 → 框架职责 → 推荐与实际接入

Employees can attach screenshots and documents to a ticket with a bounded file size.直接支持
框架职责与业务边界
File routes accept multipart uploads and enforce configured maxSize; the client enforces UI limits.
推荐使用方式
Expose uploadOne/uploadMany with a positive maxSize and associate returned file ids with the business record.
Agent 实际使用
The app uploads attachments through the file repository and shows them on ticket detail; QA uploaded a 99-byte PNG successfully.
缺口归因
未记录能力缺口
E45 E46 E64
Uploaded files must preserve metadata and be downloadable by authorized users.直接支持
框架职责与业务边界
File repository stores disk/key/filename/ext/MIME/size and exposes a content URL; business routes own access control.
推荐使用方式
Use returned ids and content URLs; put authorization on the business content route.
Agent 实际使用
The final attachment list resolves /api/service-desk/tickets/<id>/files/<id> and renders image previews/links.
缺口归因
未记录能力缺口
E47 E48 E69
Corrupt or unsupported files should be rejected with clear feedback.框架职责之外
框架职责与业务边界
The File plugin documents that it has no content sniffing; the application must validate extension, MIME and content.
推荐使用方式
Validate in the application server route or upload handler before creating metadata, with explicit user feedback.
Agent 实际使用
The app checks only count and size; QA's 28-byte corrupt.png was accepted and .exe produced a generic failure (F6).
缺口归因

未记录能力缺口
E49 E66 E64
Ticket writes and reads must remain consistent on the configured SQLite database.直接支持
框架职责与业务边界
DB transactions scope repository/query calls to the provided connection; SQLite uses a single pooled connection.
推荐使用方式
Use only the callback connection and avoid outer reads inside a transaction, especially on SQLite.
Agent 实际使用
An initial app version read manager ids inside the transaction and reopen hung; moving the read after commit fixed it (F3).
缺口归因
未记录能力缺口
E41 E49 E50 E51 E65
Tickets unresolved for over 24 hours must trigger a reminder without requiring admin-visible task UI.直接支持
框架职责与业务边界
Jobs exposes per-scope schedule executors; app-server provides the service token; the application registers the scan.
推荐使用方式
Resolve jobExecutorServiceToken, call getScheduleExecutor(scope, name), register the target and set up the executor.
Agent 实际使用
The app provider registers an overdue-scan cron through the jobs service; QA could not wait for or seed a 24-hour condition.
缺口归因
未记录能力缺口
E52 E53 E54
Acceptance needs deterministic queue state and a controllable clock/SLA to verify overdue behavior.框架职责之外
框架职责与业务边界
The factory/QA fixture owns seed volume and time control; the framework should not fake business time.
推荐使用方式
Seed at least one open ticket and one authorized engineer, and provide a clock/SLA override for the reminder window.
Agent 实际使用
Verify-3 was blocked by zero tickets, no authorized engineers and a 24-hour normal SLA.

首轮与修复过程 · 查看逐轮原始状态
轮次范围原始结论失败项
验证 1全量浏览器 QA报告未通过C01 我是IT服务台负责人，需要一个公司内部报修系统。员工提交问题、紧急程度和截图，服务台分派给工程师，工程师记录处理过程和解决办法，员工确认解决后关闭；仍未解决可以退回继续处理。 员工只看自己的工单，工程师只处理分派给自己的工单，服务台查看全部。新分派和处理结果及时通知相关人员，点击通知可以进入对应工单；超过24小时未解决时提醒服务台。首页展示待分派、超时工单和各工程师的工作量。
验证 2全量浏览器 QA报告未通过C01 我是IT服务台负责人，需要一个公司内部报修系统。员工提交问题、紧急程度和截图，服务台分派给工程师，工程师记录处理过程和解决办法，员工确认解决后关闭；仍未解决可以退回继续处理。 员工只看自己的工单，工程师只处理分派给自己的工单，服务台查看全部。新分派和处理结果及时通知相关人员，点击通知可以进入对应工单；超过24小时未解决时提醒服务台。首页展示待分派、超时工单和各工程师的工作量。
验证 3全量浏览器 QA报告未通过未记录 failed 项；不代表全部操作完成

NocoBase3 库、插件、模板与指引 4

做得好的地方
F9 · Authorization composes collection policies and record scopes without bespoke enforcement

NocoBase3 插件 · @nocobase/app-plugin-authorization · @nocobase/app-plugin-users · app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-plugin-authorization/references/business-module.md

⌄

The package resolves permission-set grants into database conditions, refuses absent policies, and exposes a repository withPolicy enforcement point. Users composes those invariants into account disable/delete transactions.

提供的帮助
Applications can implement role-scoped ticket access without duplicating authorization logic.
应保留的能力
Keep the collection-id policy contract and the explicit missing-policy denial as the documented pattern.
E39 E40 E44 E42
做得好的地方
F10 · In-app inbox provides durable user isolation and typed deep-link targets

NocoBase3 插件 · @nocobase/app-plugin-notification-in-app · app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-plugin-notification-in-app/SKILL.md

⌄

The server store scopes every operation by user id and persists a validated target; the client exposes unread count, pagination, read-state mutations and target-bearing items. The application only composes its own page and bell.

提供的帮助
Notifications remain authoritative across reconnects and can open the exact business record without custom delivery logic.
应保留的能力
Retain the explicit target contract and user-scoped store as the public integration boundary.
E58 E59 E60 E61
做得好的地方
F11 · Client application provides route, service and i18n composition in one root

NocoBase3 内核 · @nocobase/app-client · app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-plugin-i18n/SKILL.md

⌄

AppClientRoot mounts the router and Refine, while AppClientProviders composes React providers, navigation guards and route contributions. API, i18n, breadcrumb and toaster services are exported for application pages.

提供的帮助
Applications can add pages, route authorization and translations without rebuilding the shell or service wiring.
应保留的能力
Continue exposing these composition points as stable public entries.
E55 E56 E57
做得好的地方
F12 · Jobs service exposes per-scope schedule executors for quiet reminders

NocoBase3 内核 · @nocobase/jobs · @nocobase/app-server · app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-plugin-scheduler/SKILL.md

⌄

getScheduleExecutor(scope, name) caches by selection key and scope, the app-server provider registers jobExecutorServiceToken, and the scheduler skill directs non-UI tasks to this API rather than the admin-facing Scheduler plugin.

提供的帮助
The application can implement the overdue scan as background work without adding an unnecessary administrator task UI.
应保留的能力
Keep the per-scope executor API and the explicit Scheduler-versus-executor decision rule.
E52 E53 E54

问题、误导与建议统一收录在问题与改进，不在此重复展示。

本次评审的覆盖限制
  • 未执行应用、未安装依赖、未修改冻结快照；运行行为来自归档 QA 报告与原始 agent 历史。
  • 当前模型不能读取图片，因此未评审 UI 截图，也未对视觉质量评分。
  • 历史错误信号按原始 JSONL 行号逐条处置；浏览器下载超时和选择器未命中属于 QA 工具限制。
  • 评分是评审者基于已安装包 dist 实现、同步 guidance 和归档 QA 的判断，不代表上游主线分支。
  • C01 的 24 小时提醒未能在归档验收窗口内观察，属于工厂种子数据与 SLA 限制，不是已证实的框架缺陷。
  • 快照未包含 4 个超出预算、非普通文件或不可读取的文件；未据此确认其实现。
评审基线与版本指纹

评审针对已封存候选补丁与当时的依赖；可能在原搭建内或之后单独补跑。与 PR 或 NocoBase3 当前最新提交不是同一概念。评审只读冻结材料，引用运行日志不代表评审者重新复现。此报告未执行最新上游源码复核。

Resolve no application-user contact fields here.\n\nA Provider implements `NotificationProviderDefinition` with globally unique `type`, `messageType`, optional `validateConfig`, and `createProvider(context, config)`. Configuration has a `provider` discriminator and flat transport options. The runtime exposes the same `type`, `send`, optional capabilities, and optional `close`. There is no Provider instance name.\n\n```ts\nconst registry = app.container.resolve(notificationExtensionRegistryToken);\nregistry\n  .registerChannel(createSmsChannelDefinition())\n  .registerProvider(createExampleSmsProviderDefinition());\n```\n\nProvider definitions reference their reusable message handler through `messageType`. Extend `NotificationChannelSchemas` with the Provider identifier and its recipient/message schema so configured Channel names infer the correct `messages` values. Extension plugins register through `notificationExtensionRegistryToken` before activation; consumers send through `notificationServiceToken`.\n\n## Result classification\n\nReturn `accepted` only when the external service confirms it accepted the submission. Include a Provider message id when available.\n\nReturn `failed` when the service definitively rejected or did not submit the message. Classify the error and choose retry disposition:\n\n- `never` for invalid recipient/content/configuration and other permanent failures.\n- `same_provider` for bounded transient failures such as a retryable rate limit or temporary network failure.\n- Set `retryAfterMs` only from a validated Provider hint. The notification runtime uses the configured fixed retry interval when this field is absent.\n\nReturn `submission_unknown` when the request may have reached the Provider but confirmation was lost. This prevents automatic duplicates.\n\nDeclare Provider-side duplicate protection with `capabilities.idempotency`. This describes the external Provider contract only; it does not make an `unknown` Delivery directly retryable. Omitted capabilities are treated as `{ idempotency: { supported: false } }`. Set `{ supported: true }` only when repeated submissions with the same core-supplied `deliveryId` are idempotent; include `retentionMs` when the guarantee expires. The built-in database Provider is durable for the Delivery lifetime, Resend is bounded to its declared retention, and SMTP plus the built-in Webhook Providers omit capabilities because they do not claim idempotency.\n\nUse the core error categories: `authentication`, `channel`, `configuration`, `content`, `network`, `provider`, `rate_limit`, `recipient`, `storage`, `timeout`, or `unknown`. Error messages must be actionable and sanitized.\n\n## Security\n\n- Validate outbound hostnames, schemes, redirects, ports, and payload sizes for Webhook-like Providers.\n- Keep credentials in Provider configuration supplied by the host secret source.\n- Redact credentials, authorization headers, Webhook query tokens, message bodies, and personal recipient data from errors/logs.\n- Bound Provider execution with the supplied `deadline` and `AbortSignal`.\n- Avoid unbounded response bodies and parse only the fields needed to classify a result.\n\n## Extension tests\n\nCover complete message validation, native recipient expansion, atomic rejection before enqueue, final recipient validation where applicable, Provider result classification, timeout/abort, same-Provider retries, lease recovery, resource cleanup, and redacted errors. Verify globally unique Provider registration and that persisted deliveries cannot move to a replacement Channel or Provider. Run package checks and consuming application checks after changing public contracts.\n# Delivery Diagnostics\n\n## Evidence order\n\n1. Resolve the exact Notification id from the send result, business source, or the logs page an application installs from the `logs-ui` registry item.\n2. Read the Notification summary and all Deliveries.\n3. For each unexpected Delivery, record Channel, Provider identifier, status, attempt count, `nextRunAt`, and sanitized `lastError`.\n4. Read Retry Audits and Attempts in order. A Retry Audit exists for every accepted manual retry request; an Attempt exists only after Provider submission starts. Identify whether failure happened before Provider submission, during a known failed submission, or after an uncertain submission.\n5. Correlate structured server logs by Notification id, Delivery id, Attempt id, Channel, and Provider identity.\n6. Inspect effective redacted configuration and runtime registration only after fixing the failing layer in the evidence chain.\n\nThe protected logs API intentionally omits message and recipient snapshots plus lease tokens. Use server-side business context for content diagnosis; do not weaken redaction or query raw tables merely to display secrets.\n\n## Status-specific checks\n\n### Pending or processing\n\n- Confirm the application composes `JobExecutorServiceProvider` and that the `jobs` configuration Deliveries run on — `notification.jobs`, otherwise `jobs.default` — is the one every instance shares; on the built-in memory configuration each process consumes only its own Deliveries.\n- Look for `notification.delivery.enqueue_failed`; the reconciler should redispatch ready work.\n- Confirm the reconciler interval and ready batch are advancing.\n- For `retrying`, the Delivery is scheduled for retry and `nextRunAt` records its earliest execution time.\n- For a long `preparing`/`submitting` state, inspect lease heartbeat, worker health, Provider timeout, and application shutdown.\n\nAn expired preparation lease returns to pending. An expired submission lease becomes unknown because the worker may have completed the external request before losing persistence.\n\n### Failed\n\n- `recipient`: the Channel rejects the recipient, the recipient is required but missing, or the address is invalid.\n- `configuration`: definition, Provider identity, sender, or runtime configuration is missing/invalid.\n- `authentication`: Provider credentials were rejected.\n- `content`: rendered/prepared message violates Provider constraints.\n- `network`, `rate_limit`, or `timeout`: inspect the Provider result, attempt count, and `nextRunAt`.\n- `storage`: persistence or in-app inbox delivery failed.\n- `provider` or `unknown`: inspect the Provider's sanitized response and correlated logs.\n\nA Provider can request same-Provider retry. Once the configured attempt limit is exhausted, the Delivery becomes terminal failed. The runtime does not fail over to another Provider.\n\n#

E39 · packages/@nocobase/app-plugin-authorization/dist/server/database/authorizer.js:176–205

Authorizer converts an unrestricted decision into a conditional database policy with a collection id, fields and scope true. The UNRESTRICTED_ACCESS message is a reason attached to a permitted result, not a denial.

    async authorizeUnrestricted(request) {
        const resourceId = request.resource.id;
        const resolved = await this.collection(resourceId, request.action);
        if ('effect' in resolved)
            return resolved;
        const resource = resolved;
        if (!databaseCollectionFieldsKnown(resource, request.params?.fields)) {
            return this.deny('UNKNOWN_DATABASE_FIELD', `One or more requested fields are not registered for ${resourceId}`);
        }
        const conditions = {
            type: 'database',
            collection: resourceId,
            action: request.action,
            scope: true,
            fields: resource.fields,
            fieldAccess: { input: '*', output: '*' },
            allFields: true,
        };
        return {
            effect: 'conditional',
            conditions,
            reasons: [
                {
                    code: UNRESTRICTED_ACCESS,
                    message: `Unrestricted access allows ${resourceId}.${request.action}`,
                    plugin: 'database',
                },
            ],
        };
    }

E41 · packages/@nocobase/db/dist/src/repository/repository.js:36–50

Repository withPolicy normalizes the supplied policy and refuses an absent policy instead of silently running unscoped. This is the enforcement point for record-scoped service queries.

    withPolicy(policy, principal) {
        // A Repository handed out by a policy-bound Connection is still a
        // DefaultRepository, and the Connection types it as `Repository`, so this
        // method stays reachable. Replacing the binding there would hand back an
        // unrestricted Repository and undo the Connection's authorization, so
        // rebinding is refused rather than allowed to widen. Tightening further
        // is what `narrow` is for.
        if (this.options.policy) {
            invalid('INVALID_POLICY', 'This Repository already has a bound Policy. Use narrow() to tighten it; a Policy cannot be replaced.', { collection: this.options.collection });
        }
        const input = typeof policy === 'function' ? policy(principal) : policy;
        return new DefaultRepository({
            ...this.options,
            policy: normalizeRepositoryPolicy(input),
        });

E42 · packages/@nocobase/app-plugin-users/dist/server/services/users.js:118–137

Users management runs disable/remove inside a caller-owned transaction and asks the Permission Sets API whether a subject is still removable. It composes user administration with permission-set invariants.

            return this.services.users
                .withConnection(connection)
                .update(userId, input);
        });
        return this.withRoleScopes(user, this.services.database.connection());
    }
    async disable(userId) {
        const user = await this.services.database.transaction(async (connection) => {
            for (const scope of this.services.roleScopes.list()) {
                await scope.assertCanDisable?.(userId, connection);
            }
            // A disabled account can no longer act, so disabling it removes the
            // subject from every Permission Set as surely as revoking would.
            await this.services.permissionSets
                ?.withTransaction(connection)
                .assertSubjectRemovable({ type: 'user', id: userId });
            await lockUserForAdministration(connection, userId);
            return this.services.users.withConnection(connection).disable(userId);
        });
        return this.withRoleScopes(user, this.services.database.connection());

E43 · app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-plugin-users/SKILL.md:17–35

The Users guidance states that the plugin contributes only the invitation page and no user-management page, and that the application must build it on UsersClient. It also states the plugin grants no access by itself.

- Client registration factory and `UsersClientOptions`:
  `@nocobase/app-plugin-users/client`. It contributes only the public
  invitation page at `/invite/:token`; there is no user management page.
- Typed API client: `UsersClient` and its types from
  `@nocobase/app-plugin-users/client/user-client`.
- Server contracts: `userManagementServiceToken`,
  `userRoleScopeRegistryToken`, `UserRoleScope`, and related types from
  `@nocobase/app-plugin-users/server/tokens`.
- HTTP API: `GET /api/users` (`page`, `pageSize`, `q`, `status`, `roleScope`, `role`; answers `{ data, meta: { page, pageSize, total } }`), `GET /api/users/options`, `POST /api/users`, `PATCH /api/users/:userId`, `DELETE /api/users/:userId?confirm=true`, `POST /api/users/:userId/disable|enable|resetPassword|revokeSessions`, and `PUT /api/users/:userId/roleScopes/:scope`. The running application documents each one, with request and response schemas, at `/api/swagger/docs` (JSON at `/api/swagger`) under the `Users` tag. Failures use the standard error body; branch on `error.reason`, such as `USER_NOT_FOUND`, `SELF_DELETE_NOT_ALLOWED`, `LAST_ASSIGNMENT` or `USER_EMAIL_CONFLICT`.

Every HTTP route requires Authentication and Authorization. Routes check the `user` record type with one of `read`, `create`, `update`, `disable`, `enable`, `assign-role`, `reset-password`, `revoke-sessions` or `delete`. Account creation checks both `create` and `assign-role`. `delete` is permitted only while an application role scope can clean a deleted user up.

## Register the plugin

1. Register Authentication, Authorization, and then Users in the App's Client
   and Server plugin arrays. The Client factory takes no placement options:
   `users()`.
2. Grant only the `user` actions those roles need, on `{ type: 'user', id: '*' }` because `user` is a record type. The plugin creates no roles and grants no access by itself.
3. For a user management page, build it in the App on `UsersClient` and declare it among the App's own routes with the page access it needs; the plugin ships none.

E46 · packages/@nocobase/app-plugin-file/dist/server/routes.js:154–187

The upload route checks multipart content type and that a File is present, then stores it. It performs no magic-byte or MIME allowlist check.

                        if (!writable)
                            throw new Error('Upload reached its handler unauthorized.');
                        if (!c.req
                            .header('content-type')
                            ?.toLowerCase()
                            .startsWith('multipart/form-data;'))
                            throw uploadError('INVALID_ARGUMENT', 'UNSUPPORTED_MEDIA_TYPE', 'Expected multipart/form-data.', 415);
                        let body;
                        try {
                            body = await c.req.parseBody({ all: true });
                        }
                        catch (cause) {
                            throw uploadError('INVALID_ARGUMENT', 'INVALID_MULTIPART', 'Invalid multipart body.', undefined, cause);
                        }
                        const value = body.file;
                        if (action === 'uploadOne') {
                            if (!(value instanceof File))
                                throw uploadError('INVALID_ARGUMENT', 'INVALID_FILE', 'Exactly one File is required.');
                            // Every successful upload creates a file record.
                            return c.json({
                                data: decorate(await writable.uploadOne({ file: value }), getUrl, true),
                            }, 201);
                        }
                        const uploads = Array.isArray(value)
                            ? value
                            : value === undefined
                                ? []
                                : [value];
                        if (!uploads.length ||
                            !uploads.every((file) => file instanceof File))
                            throw uploadError('INVALID_ARGUMENT', 'INVALID_FILES', 'At least one File is required.');
                        return c.json({
                            data: decorate(await writable.uploadMany({ files: uploads }), getUrl, true),
                        }, 201);

原文件 SHA-256：2507ba7727a7e0e72f30924de159a45045429bd1ffdb3d16edefd030d7de6ab1

E48 · app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-plugin-file/SKILL.md:251–258

The File skill defines upload defaults and explicitly says the plugin has no built-in content sniffing or malware scan; applications must implement business validation. This makes corrupt-content acceptance an application responsibility.

- Upload defaults are 5 MiB single / 20 MiB batch for the whole multipart body, including overhead. Direct Server uploads have no HTTP limit; UI maxSize checks an individual file. The Client supplies the multipart boundary.
- Upload failures use the standard `/api` error body with domain `file`; branch on `error.reason` (`ApiClientError.reason` in the Client), never on `message`. A successful upload answers 201.
- BODY_TOO_LARGE (413) and UNSUPPORTED_MEDIA_TYPE (415): reduce request size or adjust route limits; send `multipart/form-data`. INVALID_MULTIPART/INVALID_FILE/INVALID_FILES (400): send native File values and a nonempty batch. PRINCIPAL_REQUIRED (403): a Policy function needs a resolved principal.
- INVALID_FILE_COLLECTION: first compare the migration name, route collection and Server repository argument; then await collections.get(logicalName) and check the active connection and generated metadata. SQLite physical text does not imply a broken datetime definition. Fix the specific lookup, metadata or schema problem and retry the same upload; do not bypass File Repository. Use a new migration for corrections to already-merged history.
- STORAGE_URL_UNAVAILABLE (503): configure a capable disk or stream mode.
- FILE_COMMIT_UNCERTAIN / FILE_CLEANUP_FAILED: reconcile database records and stored objects before retrying; uploads have no idempotency key.

There is no built-in route authentication, row ACL, Range/206, ETag, conditional download, resumable upload, physical cleanup, content sniffing or malware scan. Metadata deletion retains objects; cancelled forms may leave unlinked files. Implement the policies required by the business, including referenced-file deletion and orphan cleanup.

原文件 SHA-256：55b6a892b607483ed34ac272db84d06a8d6e2bdd134747b7288021cff11ba480

E49 · app/.agents/skills/nocobase-db/SKILL.md:282–299

The DB transaction guidance warns that outer db.query()/repository()/connection() calls inside a transaction do not roll back. It does not warn that on a single-connection SQLite pool such a call blocks until the pool acquire timeout and looks like a hung request.

## 5. Transactions

```ts
await db.transaction(async (connection) => {
  await connection.repository('orders').createOne({ values: order });
  await connection.query
    .updateTable('inventory')
    .set({ reserved: true })
    .where('sku', '=', order.sku)
    .execute();
});
```

Use only the `connection` the callback hands you. Calling the outer `db.query()`, `db.builder()`, `db.repository()` or `db.connection()` inside the callback runs outside the transaction and will not roll back with it — the same applies to metadata work, which must go through the transaction connection's `collectionMetadata` and `collections`.

Returning commits; throwing rolls back. Swallowing an error inside the callback and continuing commits the partial work.

A Repository nested write opens its own transaction when none is active and joins an existing one when there already is, without a savepoint per write. Several independent calls that must roll back together need an explicit transaction around them.

                try {
                    installDecimalAggregates(connection);
                    if (afterCreate) {
                        afterCreate(connection, done);
                    }
                    else
                        done(null, connection);
                }
                catch (error) {
                    done(error);
                }
            },
        };
    },

E51 · packages/@nocobase/db-sqlite/dist/src/transaction.js:1–39

SQLite patches COMMIT failure so a pooled connection is rolled back before release. This protects transaction state but does not address a nested read waiting for the same single connection.

import { createRequire } from 'node:module';
const require = createRequire(import.meta.url);
const Transaction_Sqlite = require('knex/lib/dialects/sqlite3/execution/sqlite-transaction.js');
// Knex turns a failed COMMIT into a rejection and releases the connection
// without rolling back. Servers such as PostgreSQL end the transaction
// themselves when COMMIT fails, but SQLite keeps it open so that the COMMIT
// can be retried — after a deferred foreign key violation, or SQLITE_BUSY
// while another connection reads the file. Knex never retries, so the pooled
// connection would go on running every later query inside that transaction.
class SqliteTransaction extends Transaction_Sqlite {
    commit(connection, value) {
        const query = this.trxClient.query(connection, 'COMMIT;').then((response) => {
            this._resolver(value);
            return response;
        }, (error) => {
            try {
                this.rollbackFailedCommit(connection);
            }
            finally {
                this._rejecter(error);
            }
        });
        this._completed = true;
        return query;
    }
    // Runs synchronously on the native connection, before the rejection lets
    // Knex release the connection to the next caller.
    rollbackFailedCommit(connection) {
        // The COMMIT is also rejected, without running, when the transaction was
        // already completed: the container called `trx.commit()` itself and Knex
        // then commits again. By then the connection has been released, and a
        // transaction it is now open for belongs to the next caller.
        if (connection.__knexTxId !== this.txid)
            return;
        if (!connection.inTransaction)
            return;
        try {
            connection.exec('ROLLBACK');
        }

原文件 SHA-256：b34da45b8a3862ea95d178d3461ea5fe0f5baff7e36f12b6bdc02b0778e2aaf3

E52 · app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-plugin-scheduler/SKILL.md:12–17

Scheduler guidance says non-UI recurring tasks should use an application-owned ScheduleExecutor from jobExecutorServiceToken, not the Scheduler plugin. This matches the quiet overdue reminder requirement.

## Decide Whether to Use Scheduler

The primary reason to define tasks through Scheduler is **UI observability for business administrators**. Developers define schedules and execution logic in code. Business administrators use the task list and detail pages the application builds on the Scheduler API to view schedules, track individual executions, and enable or disable tasks when authorized.

Tasks that do not need to be viewed and tracked through the UI must not be managed by the Scheduler plugin. Give recurring ones a `ScheduleExecutor` of their own from the application's jobs service (`jobExecutorServiceToken` in `@nocobase/app-server/jobs`), and one-off background work a `JobExecutor`; the application development Skill describes both. For administrator-visible tasks, deliver Client registration, access permissions, meaningful task names, and accurate final execution status together; successful server-side dispatch alone is incomplete.

E53 · packages/@nocobase/jobs/dist/service.js:17–33

The jobs service exposes getScheduleExecutor(scope, name), caches executors by selection key and scope, and resolves memory or Redis backends. Per-scope scheduled work is a first-class API.

        getScheduleExecutor(scope, name) {
            if (shutdownPromise) {
                throw new Error('The jobs service has been shut down.');
            }
            assertValidScope(scope);
            const selection = selectJobsConfig(config, name);
            // Cache identity includes the selected key. Existing Schedule queues do
            // not: equal connections, namespaces and scopes share physical firings.
            const identity = JSON.stringify([selection.key, scope]);
            const existing = executors.get(identity);
            if (existing)
                return existing;
            const resolved = resolveJobsConfig(selection, scope, dependencies);
            const executor = resolved.adapter === 'memory'
                ? factories.memory(resolved, dependencies)
                : factories.redis(resolved, dependencies);
            executors.set(identity, executor);

E54 · packages/@nocobase/app-server/dist/jobs/provider.js:16–40

The app-server jobs provider registers jobExecutorServiceToken and creates the executor service from application jobs config, giving application services a supported way to schedule background work.

export class JobExecutorServiceProvider extends ServiceProvider {
    options;
    name = '@nocobase/app-server/jobs';
    service;
    constructor(app, options = {}) {
        super(app);
        this.options = options;
    }
    register() {
        this.app.container.singleton(jobExecutorServiceToken, (container) => this.create(container));
    }
    async shutdown() {
        await this.service?.shutdown();
    }
    create(container) {
        const logger = container.has(loggingToken)
            ? container
                .resolve(loggingToken)
                .getLogger('jobs')
                .child({ module: 'jobs' })
            : undefined;
        const reportFallback = !DEVELOPMENT_ENVIRONMENTS.has(this.options.nodeEnv ?? '');
        this.service = createJobExecutorService(this.app.config.get('jobs'), {

E56 · packages/@nocobase/app-client/dist/app-client.js:8–29

AppClientRoot mounts the browser router and Refine providers; AppClientProviders composes React providers, navigation guards and route contributions. This is the supported page/routing composition layer.

export function AppClientRoot({ app }) {
    const config = app.renderConfig;
    const refine = app.refineConfig;
    const configuredChildren = refine.children === undefined ? config.routes : refine.children;
    return (_jsx(BrowserRouter, { basename: normalizeAppClientBasename(config.basename), children: _jsx(AppClientProviders, { app: app, children: configuredChildren }) }));
}
/**
 * Everything `AppClientRoot` mounts inside its router: the application, its React providers and Refine, around
 * `children` in place of the routes. It needs a router above it. A test renders a page in it under a router of its own,
 * so the page reads the services, translations and toaster it reads in the running application.
 */
export function AppClientProviders({ app, children, }) {
    const config = app.renderConfig;
    const refine = app.refineConfig;
    const configuredRouterProvider = refine.routerProvider ?? routerProvider;
    const reactProviders = config.reactProviders ?? [];
    const content = reactProviders.reduceRight((inner, ReactProvider) => _jsx(ReactProvider, { children: inner }), _jsx(Refine, { ...refine, routerProvider: configuredRouterProvider, options: {
            syncWithLocation: true,
            disableTelemetry: true,
            ...refine.options,
        }, children: children }));
    return (_jsx(ClientApplicationContext.Provider, { value: app, children: _jsx(NavigationGuardProvider, { children: content }) }));

原文件 SHA-256：ca6289408f31bd490fa736b914ccb692c7d9a8a147e466518ba683e305abdb79

E57 · app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-plugin-i18n/SKILL.md:30–38

The i18n guidance says not to build a language picker because the template already renders one, and explains namespace scope for application pages. Translation support is supplied rather than reimplemented.

- Do not build a language picker. `useAppLocale()` exists; `app-template-default` already renders one in `client/layouts/components/language-switcher.tsx`.
- Do not add i18n machinery to an application. The plugin is registered by default, the client runtime is built by `createAppI18nRuntime`, and the server's by `I18nProvider`.
- Do not configure the list of languages. It is whatever the application's own `locales/index.ts` declares; configuration names only the default, `i18n.defaultLocale` (overridden by `APP_DEFAULT_LOCALE`). The `@nocobase/app-plugin-i18n` README covers that, `useAppLocale()`, the endpoints, and `useSyncServerLocale()`, which the template shell already calls to keep the server's language in step with the browser's.

# The one rule that decides everything

**A namespace is a package name, and the namespace in scope follows the render tree.**

An application's pages get the application's namespace; a plugin's pages get that plugin's, because the host wraps each contribution using the `packageName` it already records. So a component in its own package's routes translates with no namespace at all.

原文件 SHA-256：12afdb44486ee0f5c1d601ea0f1aa933b00b2ebefdf3cfc383e54f909963c9c0

E58 · app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-plugin-notification-in-app/SKILL.md:18–31

The in-app notification skill states that the plugin owns persistence, APIs and client inbox components, while the application owns its page and route. It does not promise a header bell or a notifications page.

This Skill is published with `@nocobase/app-plugin-notification-in-app`. The plugin owns its Server runtime, persistence, APIs, realtime contract, and Client inbox components. The target application owns whether both Client and Server entries are registered.

# Scope

- Register the Client and Server plugin entries and the core notification dependency.
- Render the exported inbox components in an application-owned page.
- Consume the public realtime topic and event types from the `/realtime` package entry.
- Verify or diagnose inbox HTTP access, mutations, user isolation, and reconnect refresh.

# Non-Goals

- Do not replace durable notifications with transient browser toasts.
- Do not edit inbox tables directly or publish forged realtime events as notification state.
- Do not expect the plugin to contribute an inbox page; the application owns the route.

E59 · packages/@nocobase/app-plugin-notification-in-app/dist/client/api.d.ts:1–31

The client API exposes inbox items with an optional target, unread count and read/unread/delete mutations. These are the primitives needed for a bell and click-through.

import type { NotificationTarget } from '@nocobase/app-plugin-notification/client';
import type { ApiClient } from '@nocobase/app-client';
export type InboxMutationAction = 'read' | 'unread' | 'delete';
export interface InboxItem {
    readonly id: string;
    readonly deliveryId: string;
    readonly notificationId: string;
    readonly title: string;
    readonly body: string;
    readonly target?: NotificationTarget;
    readonly readAt?: string;
    readonly createdAt: string;
}
export interface InboxFilters {
    readonly unreadOnly?: boolean;
    readonly pageSize?: number;
    /** The `nextPageToken` of the previous page, passed back unchanged. */
    readonly pageToken?: string;
}
export interface InboxListResponse {
    readonly data: readonly InboxItem[];
    /** Present while more messages follow; pass it back as `pageToken`. */
    readonly nextPageToken?: string;
}
export declare function fetchInbox(client: ApiClient, filters: InboxFilters, signal?: AbortSignal): Promise<InboxListResponse>;
export declare function fetchUnreadCount(client: ApiClient, signal?: AbortSignal): Promise<number>;
/** Marks one message read or unread, returning it, or deletes it, returning nothing. */
export declare function mutateInboxItem(client: ApiClient, id: string, action: 'read' | 'unread'): Promise<InboxItem>;
export declare function mutateInboxItem(client: ApiClient, id: string, action: 'delete'): Promise<undefined>;
export declare function mutateInboxItem(client: ApiClient, id: string, action: InboxMutationAction): Promise<InboxItem | undefined>;
export declare function markInboxRead(client: ApiClient): Promise<number>;

E60 · packages/@nocobase/app-plugin-notification-in-app/dist/client/api.js:1–47

The client implementation calls the authenticated notificationInApp/messages endpoints, reads paginated data and unread counts, and mutates read state. The browser does not reconstruct an API base.

const MESSAGES_PATH = 'notificationInApp/messages';
export async function fetchInbox(client, filters, signal) {
    const query = new URLSearchParams({
        pageSize: String(filters.pageSize ?? 25),
    });
    if (filters.unreadOnly)
        query.set('unreadOnly', 'true');
    if (filters.pageToken)
        query.set('pageToken', filters.pageToken);
    const value = await client.request({
        path: `${MESSAGES_PATH}?${query}`,
        signal,
    });
    if (isRecord(value) && Array.isArray(value.data)) {
        const meta = isRecord(value.meta) ? value.meta : {};
        return {
            data: value.data,
            ...(typeof meta.nextPageToken === 'string'
                ? { nextPageToken=[REDACTED] }
                : {}),
        };
    }
    throw new Error('Inbox returned an invalid response.');
}
export async function fetchUnreadCount(client, signal) {
    const response = await client.request({
        path: `${MESSAGES_PATH}/unreadCount`,
        signal,
    });
    return response.data.count;
}
export async function mutateInboxItem(client, id, action) {
    const path = `${MESSAGES_PATH}/${encodeURIComponent(id)}`;
    if (action === 'delete') {
        await mutation(client, path, 'DELETE');
        return undefined;
    }
    const response = await mutation(client, `${path}/${action === 'read' ? 'markRead' : 'markUnread'}`, 'POST');
    return response.data;
}
export async function markInboxRead(client) {
    const response = await mutation(client, `${MESSAGES_PATH}/markAllRead`, 'POST');
    return response.data.updated;
}
async function mutation(client, path, method) {
    // Cross-site writes are rejected by the authentication plugin's origin check, so no CSRF token is sent.
    return client.request({ path, method });

E61 · packages/@nocobase/app-plugin-notification-in-app/dist/server/store.js:108–150

The server store scopes every list, unread count and mutation by userId, persists target, and keeps HTTP state authoritative. This supports user-isolated durable notifications.

    async list(input) {
        let query = this.database
            .query()
            .selectFrom('notificationInAppItems')
            .selectAll()
            .where('userId', '=', input.userId)
            .orderBy('createdAt', 'desc')
            .orderBy('id', 'desc')
            .limit(input.limit ?? 25);
        if (input.unreadOnly)
            query = query.where('readAt', 'is', null);
        if (input.before)
            query = query.where((builder) => builder.or([
                builder('createdAt', '<', input.before?.createdAt),
                builder.and([
                    builder('createdAt', '=', input.before?.createdAt),
                    builder('id', '<', input.before?.id),
                ]),
            ]));
        return (await query.execute()).map(fromRow);
    }
    async countUnread(userId) {
        const rows = await this.database
            .query()
            .selectFrom('notificationInAppItems')
            .select(({ fn }) => [fn.countAll().as('count')])
            .where('userId', '=', userId)
            .where('readAt', 'is', null)
            .executeTakeFirst();
        return Number(rows?.count ?? 0);
    }
    async update(input) {
        const now = new Date().toISOString();
        if (input.action === 'delete') {
            const current = await this.database
                .query()
                .selectFrom('notificationInAppItems')
                .selectAll()
                .where('id', '=', input.id)
                .where('userId', '=', input.userId)
                .executeTakeFirst();
            if (!current)
                return undefined;

E67 · app/server/service-desk/service.ts:956–972

The final service policy helper indexes decision.conditions.database by the real collection names serviceDeskTickets and serviceDeskComments, and denies when no policy is present.

  private policy(
    decision: AuthorizationDecision,
    collection: 'serviceDeskTickets' | 'serviceDeskComments',
  ): RepositoryPolicy {
    const database = (
      decision.conditions as
        | { database?: Record<string, RepositoryPolicy | false | undefined> }
        | undefined
    )?.database;
    const policy = database?.[collection];
    if (!policy) throw new AuthorizationDeniedError(decision);
    return policy;
  }

  private ticketPolicy(decision: AuthorizationDecision): RepositoryPolicy {
    return this.policy(decision, 'serviceDeskTickets');
  }

E68 · app/client/components/notification-bell.tsx:33–54

The application-owned bell composes inbox hooks, an unread count, mark-read behavior and navigation to the stored target or /inbox. The plugin supplies the hooks, not the header component.

export function NotificationBell(): ReactElement {
  const { t, i18n } = useTranslation();
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const [open, setOpen] = useState(false);
  const items = useInboxItems(PREVIEW_SIZE);
  const unread = useInboxUnreadCount();
  const actions = useInboxActions();
  useInboxRefresh();

  const unreadCount = unread.data ?? 0;
  const latest = (items.data?.pages.flatMap((page) => page.data) ?? []).slice(
    0,
    PREVIEW_SIZE,
  );

  const openItem = (id: string, path: string | null): void => {
    setOpen(false);
    // Marking read is best effort: the message still opens even if the
    // bookkeeping call fails, and the inbox refetches on its own.
    void actions.mark(id, 'read').catch(() => undefined);
    void navigate(path ?? '/inbox');
  };

E71 · packages/@nocobase/app-plugin-authorization/dist/server/database/api.js:20–34

policyFor builds a database.collection resource with the collection id and folds read/create/update/delete decisions. Database policy keys are therefore collection ids, not an arbitrary composite logical name.

    async policyFor(collection, context, operation) {
        const resource = { type: 'database.collection', id: collection };
        const decide = async (action) => foldDecision(action, await context.authorize({
            resource,
            action,
            params: operation ? { operation } : {},
        }));
        const [read, create, update, remove] = await Promise.all([
            decide('read'),
            decide('create'),
            decide('update'),
            decide('delete'),
        ]);
        return { read, create, update, delete: remove };
    }

E73 · packages/@nocobase/db/dist/src/database/internal/knex/client.js:1–20

The common db client builds the knex pool by calling the dialect's configurePool(config, config.pool ?? {}), or uses config.pool ?? {} directly. With SQLite's configurePool present and no pool in the application config, the wrapper supplies no size or acquire timeout of its own; the underlying knex SQLite defaults remain.

import knex from 'knex';
export function createKnexClient(config) {
    const baseClient = config.databaseDriver?.resolveKnexClient?.();
    const dialectClient = config.databaseDriver?.createKnexClient?.(config, baseClient);
    const client = knex({
        client: dialectClient ?? baseClient ?? config.knexClient,
        connection: config.connection,
        pool: config.databaseDriver?.configurePool
            ? config.databaseDriver.configurePool(config, config.pool ?? {})
            : resolvePoolConfig(config),
        useNullAsDefault: config.useNullAsDefault,
        searchPath: config.searchPath,
        debug: config.debug,
    });
    // Keep dialect detection stable when the driver uses a local subclass.
    client.client.config.client = config.knexClient;
    return client;
}
function resolvePoolConfig(config) {
    return config.pool ?? {};

E75 · app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-development/references/database-connections.md:34–54

The database-connections reference documents the template's SQLite default and SQLite filename/database path rules, but does not mention a single-connection pool, waiting for the transaction's connection, acquire timeouts or hung requests.

Start by reading `server/config/database.ts` and the connection structure in `config.example.yml`. The template's default connection is `main` on SQLite. Preserve other connections and driver registrations when changing one connection.

| Location                    | Responsibility                                                                                                                        |
| --------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `server/config/database.ts` | Declare connection defaults with `defineAppDatabaseConfig`; optionally register explicit drivers; map environment variables.          |
| `config.yml`                | Override connection settings for the environment, including credentials. This file is gitignored.                                     |
| `config.example.yml`        | Document the supported connection structure and credential placeholders for future installations. Keep it aligned with code defaults. |

The callback passed to `defineAppDatabaseConfig` receives the runtime context and runs during configuration resolution, not at module import. When supplied, explicit drivers determine the accepted dialects and connection fields in TypeScript. Without registrations, use a driver-owned connection type with `satisfies` when strict dialect-specific checking is needed. YAML overrides are resolved at runtime; passing `pnpm typecheck` does not validate their contents or prove connectivity.

The second argument of `defineAppDatabaseConfig` declares the section's environment variables. The templates pass `{ env: connectionEnvironment('main') }`, which maps `DB_DIALECT`, `DB_HOST`, `DB_PORT`, `DB_DATABASE`, `DB_USERNAME`, `DB_PASSWORD`, `DB_SSL` and `DB_FILENAME` onto `connections.main`, above `config.yml`. A connection you add declares its own with a prefix naming it, merged into the same `env`: `{ ...connectionEnvironment('main'), ...connectionEnvironment('analytics', 'DB_ANALYTICS') }`. For SQLite, a `database` alias in `config.yml` still takes precedence over `DB_FILENAME`.

`config.yml` deep-merges into code defaults. Changing a dialect in code does not remove an old YAML override or fields belonging to the previous dialect. Update both layers when switching, and check any existing environment overrides using the Configuration section of `README.MD` in the application root.

Official drivers are optional peers of `@nocobase/app-server`. Install the needed package in application `dependencies` and configure its dialect; no driver import or registration is required. The application runtime awaits `resolveDatabaseConfig()` after configuration and defaults are merged, before provider registration. It imports only configured dialects and does not open connections. The core manager stays synchronous and requires prepared or explicitly registered drivers. YAML cannot install packages. Explicit `drivers` and connection-level `databaseDriver` values retain precedence and conflict checking. Custom dialects require explicit registration; registration keys must match their driver's dialect.

### SQLite paths

TypeScript connections require `filename`, normally `runtime.paths.storage('database.sqlite')`. YAML also accepts `database: database.sqlite`: the SQLite driver resolves it under the application's storage directory. Absolute paths remain absolute, and `:memory:` is preserved.

The YAML `database` alias takes precedence over `filename` during normalization. If changing `filename` appears to have no effect, check for an existing `database` override. Use `filename` in typed code; the YAML alias is not part of `SqliteConnectionConfig`.

原文件 SHA-256：5f6541bffb6b943ee87df64b5e3bde840e8bace38458d8e6d2048eadc60590d0

What was delivered

业务场景与交付说明

报修与分派

员工可填写标题、描述、优先级并上传截图；服务台可将工单分派给指定工程师，状态自动进入处理中。

处理与退回

工程师可发布处理过程评论、填写解决方案并解决工单；员工可附原因退回，工程师再次处理并解决。

确认关闭与通知

员工确认解决后工单关闭；分派、评论、解决和关闭通知均出现在通知列表，点击标题可进入对应工单。

权限与首页

员工只看到自己的工单，工程师只看到分派给自己的工单，服务台查看全部；首页展示待分派、超时和工程师工作量。

01员工提交问题、优先级和截图
→
02服务台分派给工程师
→
03工程师记录处理过程与解决办法
→
04员工确认关闭或退回
→
05退回后工程师再次处理
→
06员工再次确认后关闭
→
07各角色通知点击进入工单
→
08服务台首页查看待分派、超时与工作量
